SELECT [b].[Id], [b].[Address], [b].[Capacity], [b].[CategoryId], [b].[CheckInTime], [b].[CheckOutTime], [b].[CreatedDate], [b].[Description], [b].[District], [b].[Latitude], [b].[Location], [b].[Longitude], [b].[MinNights], [b].[Name], [b].[Neighborhood], [b].[PricePerNight], [b].[Province], [b].[SizeM2], [b].[Status], [c].[Id], [c].[CategoryName], [c].[CreatedDate], [c].[Description], [c].[ImageUrl], [c].[Status], [b0].[Id], [b0].[BungalowId], [b0].[CreatedDate], [b0].[ImageUrl], [b0].[Status], [t].[AmenitiesId], [t].[BungalowsId], [t].[Id], [t].[CreatedDate], [t].[IconCode], [t].[Name], [t].[Status]
FROM [Bungalows] AS [b]
INNER JOIN [Categories] AS [c] ON [b].[CategoryId] = [c].[Id]
LEFT JOIN [BungalowImages] AS [b0] ON [b].[Id] = [b0].[BungalowId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b1].[AmenitiesId], [b1].[BungalowsId], [a].[Id], [a].[CreatedDate], [a].[IconCode], [a].[Name], [a].[Status]
    FROM [BungalowAmenities] AS [b1]
    INNER JOIN [Amenities] AS [a] ON [b1].[AmenitiesId] = [a].[Id]
) AS [t] ON [b].[Id] = [t].[BungalowsId]
ORDER BY [b].[Id], [c].[Id], [b0].[Id], [t].[AmenitiesId], [t].[BungalowsId]
